# Lineage

Generated by `extract.py report` on 2026-11-09 from 1777 full extractions. Each line is what a paper says about earlier work, with the relation the reader recorded. A linked key is a reading we hold.

## Disagreements

- Girshick 2014, *Rich Feature Hierarchies for Accurate Object Detection and Semantic Segmentation* contradicts Szegedy et al. DNN detection: regression-based localisation reaches 30.5%
- Sundararajan 2017, *Axiomatic Attribution for Deep Networks* contradicts DeepLift (Shrikumar et al.): Shown to break Implementation Invariance
- Sundararajan 2017, *Axiomatic Attribution for Deep Networks* contradicts LRP (Binder et al.): Breaks Implementation Invariance
- Sundararajan 2017, *Axiomatic Attribution for Deep Networks* contradicts Guided back-propagation (Springenberg et al.): Breaks Sensitivity
- Sundararajan 2017, *Axiomatic Attribution for Deep Networks* contradicts Deconvolutional networks (Zeiler & Fergus): Breaks Sensitivity
- Goyal 2017, *Accurate, Large Minibatch SGD: Training ImageNet in 1 Hour* contradicts Keskar et al., On large-batch training: paper finds optimization, not generalization, is the issue
- Prokhorenkova 2017, *CatBoost: unbiased boosting with categorical features* contradicts Breiman, Using iterated bagging to debias regressions: Appendix E shows its out-of-bag residuals are still shifted
- Howard 2018, *Universal Language Model Fine-tuning for Text Classification* contradicts Mou et al., How transferable are neural networks in NLP applications: fine-tuning shown to fail between unrelated NLP tasks; ULMFiT argues it works with proper technique
- ?, *BERT: Pre-training of Deep Bidirectional Transformers for Language Understanding* contradicts Peters et al. 2018b; Melamud et al. 2016: earlier mixed results on scaling feature-based bi-LMs; BERT argues fine-tuning benefits from size
- Shallue 2018, *Measuring the Effects of Data Parallelism on Neural Network Training* contradicts Chen et al. 2018 (critical batch size): width claim not confirmed; narrow Transformers scaled better
- Shallue 2018, *Measuring the Effects of Data Parallelism on Neural Network Training* contradicts Keskar et al. 2017 (sharp minima): large-batch generalisation gap attributed to protocol and tuning
- Hudson 2019, *GQA: A New Dataset for Real-World Visual Reasoning and Compositional Question Answering* contradicts VQA 1.0 / VQA 2.0 (Antol; Goyal et al.): argues they are prior-biased; patterns reused from VQA1.0
- Gehrmann 2019, *GLTR: Statistical Detection and Visualization of Generated Text* contradicts Lavergne et al.: Detecting fake content with relative entropy scoring: Found the opposite entropy direction
- Su 2019, *VL-BERT: Pre-training of Generic Visual-Linguistic Representations* contradicts ViLBERT (`1908.02265`): Two-stream claimed superior; VL-BERT says unified model outperforms and matching task does not help
- Lan 2019, *ALBERT: A Lite BERT for Self-supervised Learning of Language Representations* contradicts Universal Transformer (Dehghani et al.): UT shows sharing beats vanilla transformer; ALBERT sees a cost
- Lan 2019, *ALBERT: A Lite BERT for Self-supervised Learning of Language Representations* contradicts Deep Equilibrium Models (Bai et al.): ALBERT embeddings oscillate instead of converging
- A 2020, *Scaling Laws for Neural Language Models* contradicts Deep Learning Scaling is Predictable, Empirically (Hestness et al.): found super-linear dataset scaling with model size; this paper finds sub-linear
- Adiwardana 2020, *Towards a Human-like Open-Domain Chatbot* contradicts Liu et al. 2016; Lowe et al. 2017: found poor BLEU-human correlation; this paper finds perplexity correlates
- Clark 2020, *TyDi QA: A Benchmark for Information-Seeking Question Answering in Typologically Diverse Languages* contradicts MLQA: Translated/model-matched multilingual QA; vulnerable to Translationese
- Clark 2020, *TyDi QA: A Benchmark for Information-Seeking Question Answering in Typologically Diverse Languages* contradicts XQuAD: Translated SQuAD; higher lexical overlap
- Gururangan 2020, *Don’t Stop Pretraining: Adapt Language Models to Domains and Tasks* contradicts Peters et al. (2019) MultiNLI JSD finding: found term-distribution divergence does not predict performance; authors call it possibly isolated
- Roller 2021, *Recipes for Building an Open-Domain Chatbot* contradicts The curious case of neural text degeneration (Holtzman et al.) (`1904.09751`): finds tuned beam search can beat sampling
- ?, *Does the Whole Exceed its Parts? The Effect of AI Explanations on Complementary Team Performance* contradicts Lai and Tan; Lai et al. deceptive review studies: Explanations help there only because AI far outperforms the team
- ?, *Does the Whole Exceed its Parts? The Effect of AI Explanations on Complementary Team Performance* contradicts Zhang et al., confidence and explanation on income prediction: AI slightly better than best team
- ?, *Principles and Algorithms for Forecasting Groups of Time Series: Locality and Globality* contradicts Bandara, Bergmeir, Smyl, clustering with RNNs: argues clusters of similar series help; paper says clustering is not essential
- Nangia 2020, *CrowS-Pairs: A Challenge Dataset for Measuring Social Biases in Masked Language Models* contradicts StereoSet (Nadeem et al.): concurrent dataset; authors argue theirs is more valid (80% vs 62%) and criticise its p(M|U) metric
- Fedus 2021, *Switch Transformers: Scaling to Trillion Parameter Models with Simple and Efficient Sparsity* contradicts Shazeer et al. 2017 claim that k>1 is needed for routing gradients (`1701.06538`): Switch finds k=1 preserves quality
- Brock 2021, *High-Performance Large-Scale Image Recognition Without Normalization* contradicts ViT (Dosovitskiy et al.): large weight decay and Adam findings in pre-training
- Reynolds 2021, *Prompt Programming for Large Language Models: Beyond the Few-Shot Paradigm* contradicts Language models are few-shot learners (GPT-3) (`2005.14165`): disputes the meta-learning reading of few-shot; reproduces its translation prompts
- Lu 2022, *Fantastically Ordered Prompts and Where to Find Them: Overcoming Few-Shot Prompt Order Sensitivity* contradicts What makes good in-context examples for GPT-3?: Liu et al. concluded order has little effect after nearest-neighbour retrieval; this paper comes to the opposite conclusion
- Patterson 2021, *Carbon Emissions and Large Neural Network Training* contradicts Energy and policy considerations for deep learning in NLP (`1906.02243`): Revises its Evolved Transformer NAS estimate down 88X
- Kiela 2021, *Dynabench: Rethinking Benchmarking in NLP* contradicts Huang et al. counterfactually-augmented SNLI: model-in-the-loop augmented data does not necessarily generalize better
- Perez 2021, *True Few-Shot Learning with Language Models* contradicts Making pre-trained language models better few-shot learners (Gao et al.): multi-distribution-style hyperparameter choice, comparisons
- Ding 2021, *CogView: Mastering Text-to-Image Generation via Transformers* contradicts DALL-E text-loss weighting (`2102.12092`): CogView finds text modelling key; DALL-E lowers text weight
- ?, *Revisiting the Calibration of Modern Neural Networks* contradicts On calibration of modern neural networks (Guo et al.) (`1706.04599`): Trend of larger, more accurate models being worse calibrated does not continue; also source of temperature scaling
- Chen 2021, *Evaluating Large Language Models Trained on Code* contradicts BLEU / CodeBLEU (Ren et al.) (`2009.10297`): argues match-based metrics poorly track functional correctness
- Webson 2022, *Do Prompt-Based Models Really Understand the Meaning of Their Prompts?* contradicts Natural Instructions (Mishra et al.) (`2104.08773`): contradicts claim that models benefit from elaborate instructions
- Sanh 2021, *Multitask Prompted Training Enables Zero-Shot Task Generalization* contradicts Finetuned language models are zero-shot learners (FLAN) (`2109.01652`): concurrent; FLAN found multitask prompting hurt at 8B, T0 finds gains at 3B
- Askell 2021, *A General Language Assistant as a Laboratory for Alignment* contradicts Training verifiers to solve math word problems (`2110.14168`): verifier scaling advantage not seen on code, attributed to regime difference
- ?, *High-Resolution Image Synthesis with Latent Diffusion Models* contradicts Vahdat et al., LSGM: joint training of autoencoder and score prior needs delicate weighting; LDM outperforms
- ?, *To SMOTE, or not to SMOTE?* contradicts Kovacs 2019, empirical comparison of minority oversampling techniques: Source of 104 datasets; its gains attributed to weak classifiers and a-priori HPs
- Hoffmann 2022, *Training Compute-Optimal Large Language Models* contradicts Scaling Laws for Neural Language Models (Kaplan et al.) (`2001.08361`): Hoffmann finds equal scaling vs Kaplan's N ∝ C^0.73
- Tay 2022, *UL2: Unifying Language Learning Paradigms* contradicts Wettig et al. (masking rates): UL2 slightly conflicts with its high-corruption benefit finding
- Liu 2022, *Few-Shot Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning is Better and Cheaper than In-Context Learning* contradicts Compacter (`2106.04647`): does not outperform full fine-tuning here
- Workshop 2022, *BLOOM: A 176B-Parameter Open-Access Multilingual Language Model* contradicts Raffel et al., 2020: text notes decoder-only choice is opposed to T5's encoder-decoder finding for transfer learning
- Taylor 2022, *Galactica: A Large Language Model for Science* contradicts Scaling laws and interpretability of learning from repeated data (Hernandez et al.): repeated tokens found harmful there; not here
- Schuurmans 2023, *Memory Augmented Large Language Models are Computationally Universal* contradicts Perez et al. 2019; Siegelmann and Sontag; Weiss et al. 2018; Bhattamishra et al. 2020; Wei et al. 2022a: Prior universality results need modified weights/high precision; this uses frozen weights plus external memory.
- Chan 2023, *Harms from Increasingly Agentic Algorithmic Systems* contradicts Cooper et al. 2022; Nissenbaum 1996; Wieringa 2020: authors argue against the view that developers have full control, while keeping human responsibility
- Chan 2023, *Harms from Increasingly Agentic Algorithmic Systems* contradicts Leufer 2020 (AI myths): agree on responsibility, but reach opposite conclusion that AI agency is real
- ?, *Segment Anything (SAM)* contradicts On the Opportunities and Risks of Foundation Models [8]: authors note most capability comes from supervised, not self-supervised, training
- Deshpande 2023, *Toxicity in chatgpt: Analyzing persona-assigned language models* contradicts Exploring AI ethics of ChatGPT: a diagnostic analysis: Zhuo et al. found low toxicity; this work finds persona-assigned toxicity
- Li 2023, *ChatGPT for Information Extraction* contradicts Wei et al. 2023 / Gao et al. 2023 (ChatGPT for IE): they found ChatGPT desirable on IE with small random samples and domain-specific prompts
- Schaeffer 2023, *Are Emergent Abilities of LLMs a Mirage?* contradicts Emergent abilities of large language models (Wei et al.) (`2206.07682`): definition and claims of emergence being tested
- Schaeffer 2023, *Are Emergent Abilities of LLMs a Mirage?* contradicts Predictability and surprise in large generative models (Ganguli et al.): claimed emergence at arithmetic and MMLU
- Schaeffer 2023, *Are Emergent Abilities of LLMs a Mirage?* contradicts Broken neural scaling laws (Caballero et al.): they treat emergence as real via piecewise power law
- Huang 2023, *C-Eval: A Multi-Level Multi-Discipline Chinese Evaluation Suite for Foundation Models* contradicts Vicuna / Stanford Alpaca claims: finds 10B-scale instruction-tuned models stay under 40%, contradicting claims of ChatGPT parity
- Peng 2023, *Reinventing RNNs for the Transformer Era (RWKV)* contradicts Scaling laws for neural language models (Kaplan et al.) (`2001.08361`): Kaplan claimed LSTMs do not follow transformer log-log scaling; RWKV does
- Gudibande 2023, *The False Promise of Imitating Proprietary LLMs* contradicts Stanford Alpaca (Taori et al.): imitation near-parity claims
- Gudibande 2023, *The False Promise of Imitating Proprietary LLMs* contradicts Vicuna (Chiang et al.): claims 90% of ChatGPT quality; GPT-4 eval procedure reused
- Gudibande 2023, *The False Promise of Imitating Proprietary LLMs* contradicts Self-Instruct (Wang et al.): synthetic data generation; near-parity claims
- Chen 2023, *PaLI-X: On Scaling up a Multilingual Vision and Language Model* contradicts Unified-IO: argued multitask-vs-single-task gap; PaLI-X finds on-par
- Mukherjee 2023, *Imitating Reasoning Process of Larger LLMs (Orca)* contradicts The false promise of imitating proprietary LLMs (Gudibande et al.): Orca argues imitation conditions (scale, diversity) are attainable
- Dettmers 2023, *Sparse-Quantized Representation (SpQR)* contradicts Yao et al. comprehensive PTQ study: they recommend against small groups; SpQR uses tiny groups with quantized statistics
- Gandhi 2023, *Understanding Theory-of-Mind in LLMs with LLMs* contradicts Sap et al. Neural theory-of-mind?: methodology criticised (SocialIQA, ToMi)
- Gandhi 2023, *Understanding Theory-of-Mind in LLMs with LLMs* contradicts Shapira et al. Clever Hans or neural theory of mind?: authors attribute failures to missing perceptual-access information
- Lee 2023, *Teaching Arithmetic to Small Transformers* contradicts Razeghi et al., frequency effects on numerical reasoning: They link performance to number frequency; here the model adds numbers never seen in training
- Zheng 2023, *Secrets of RLHF in LLMs* contradicts Engstrom et al., Implementation matters in deep policy gradients: finds reward scaling does not help here
- Wang 2023, *SciBench: Evaluating College-Level Scientific Problem-Solving Abilities of Large Language Models* contradicts Chain-of-thought prompting (Wei et al.) (`2201.11903`): finds CoT does not universally improve skills
- Ye 2023, *FLASK* contradicts WizardLM (Xu et al.) (`2304.12244`): finds complex instructions' effect not significant vs Vicuna
- Li 2023, *SEED-Bench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs with Generative Comprehension* contradicts MMBench (`2307.06281`): Criticises ChatGPT-based answer matching (87.0% alignment) and small size
- Li 2023, *Humpback (Self-Alignment with Instruction Backtranslation)* contradicts LIMA (`2305.11206`): contrasts superficial alignment hypothesis; also baseline
- Dong 2023, *DreamLLM: Synergistic Multimodal Comprehension and Creation* contradicts Emu (Sun et al.): aligns outputs to CLIP and needs second-stage SD fine-tune
- Dong 2023, *DreamLLM: Synergistic Multimodal Comprehension and Creation* contradicts GILL (Koh et al.): CLIP-alignment baseline
- Xu 2023, *Retrieval Meets Long-Context LLMs* contradicts LongBench: Concurrent work finding retrieval helps only weak long-context models; also source of 3 datasets
- Zhu 2023, *Hypothesis Search (LLMs Can Learn Rules)* contradicts Rethinking the role of demonstrations (Min et al.): random labels do not hurt there, but random rules hurt here
- Chen 2023, *RAG for Long-Form QA* contradicts Krishna et al. 2021 Hurdles to progress in LFQA (`2103.06332`): found random and relevant docs make no difference for smaller fine-tuned models; here LLMs distinguish them
- Singh 2023, *Beyond Human Data (ReST-EM)* contradicts Scaling relationship on learning mathematical reasoning (RFT), Yuan et al.: RFT saw diminishing returns with model size; here gains grow with size
- Aksitov 2023, *ReST Meets ReAct* contradicts Large language models cannot self-correct reasoning yet (Huang et al.): self-critique helps slightly here in a process-based setup
- Zheng 2024, *SeeAct (GPT-4V as Generalist Web Agent)* contradicts Set-of-mark prompting (Yang et al.): image annotation effective on object-centric images but not on webpage screenshots here
- Merrill 2024, *The Illusion of State in State-Space Models* contradicts Combining recurrent, convolutional, and continuous-time models with linear state space layers: Lemma 3.2 claim that SSMs simulate RNNs relied on infinite depth
- Machlab 2024, *LM In-Context Recall is Prompt Dependent* contradicts Mistral 7B (`2310.06825`): Mistral paper claims it beats Llama 2 13B on all benchmarks; here v0.1 is the worst model on all three tests
- Bertsch 2024, *In-Context Learning with Long-Context Models* contradicts Levy et al. 2024: more input can reduce performance; not observed here
- Li 2024, *RAG vs. Long-Context LLMs* contradicts Retrieval meets long context large language models (Xu et al.) (`2310.03025`): found LC lags RAG; this paper finds opposite, possibly due to stronger LLMs and longer contexts; chunking follows it
- Muennighoff 2024, *OLMoE* contradicts Zhou et al. Expert Choice routing: TC with dropless beats EC here
- Muennighoff 2024, *OLMoE* contradicts Komatsuzaki et al. Sparse upcycling: Finds from-scratch catches up at 25% rather than 120% of dense compute
- Kumar 2024, *Training LLMs to Self-Correct via RL* contradicts Teaching large language models to reason with reinforcement learning (Havrilla et al.): found STaR and on-policy RL converge similarly in single-turn; SCoRe finds STaR much worse in the multi-turn case
- Hosseini 2024, *Not All LLM Reasoners Are Created Equal* contradicts Measuring and narrowing the compositionality gap in language models (`2210.03350`): Press et al. found gap does not shrink with GPT-3 size; here frontier models show smaller gaps
- Chen 2024, *ScienceAgentBench* contradicts The AI Scientist (`2408.06292`): disputes claims of end-to-end automation
- Mirzadeh 2024, *GSM-Symbolic* contradicts Ivanova et al. 2025: statistical reanalysis arguing observations are not significant; addressed in App B
- Jin 2024, *Long-Context LLMs Meet RAG* contradicts Retrieval meets long context LLMs (Xu et al.) (`2310.03025`): paper says more passages do not consistently help, contrary to Xu et al. and Li et al.
- Jin 2024, *Long-Context LLMs Meet RAG* contradicts Retrieval augmented generation or long-context LLMs? (Li et al.) (`2407.16833`): as above
- Chan 2024, *MLE-Bench* contradicts Weco AIDE report: claims >50% of humans beaten; MLE-bench finds ~10% above median
- Wu 2024, *Janus* contradicts Chameleon (`2405.09818`): Single VQ tokenizer for both tasks; Janus ablation Exp-A follows this and shows poor understanding
- Miller 2024, *A Statistical Approach to LLM Evaluation* contradicts The Llama 3 Herd of Models: its SE_Bernoulli confidence intervals are too wide or too narrow in some cases
- Huang 2024, *OpenCoder* contradicts DeepSeek-Coder: Claims repo-level dedup; OpenCoder finds file-level better
- Leto 2024, *Toward Optimal Search and Retrieval for RAG* contradicts The power of noise: Redefining retrieval for RAG systems (`2401.14887`): finds no noise setting above gold ceiling
- Zhou 2024, *PAE (Proposer-Agent-Evaluator)* contradicts Pan et al. 2024 (step-based autonomous evaluators): step-based evaluator performed worse than outcome evaluator in PAE ablation
- OLMo 2024, *OLMo 2* contradicts McCandlish et al., empirical model of large-batch training: results counter the folk wisdom that area under the learning curve matters
- Ye 2025, *LIMO: Less Is More for Reasoning* contradicts SFT memorizes, RL generalizes: Cited with the assumption that SFT mainly memorizes
- Cuadron 2025, *The Danger of Overthinking* contradicts Do not think that much for 2+3=? (Chen et al.) (`2412.21187`): they suggest token usage indicates overthinking; here more reasoning tokens lowered overthinking
- Wang 2025, *RL for Reasoning in LLMs with One Training Example* contradicts Grokking (Power et al.): argues post-saturation generalization is distinct from grokking
- Laban 2025, *LLMs Get Lost in Multi-Turn Conversation* contradicts MT-bench and follow-ups (MT-bench-101, MT-Eval, MultiChallenge, MINT) (`2306.05685`): authors argue episodic multi-turn tasks overestimate multi-turn performance
- Zhang 2025, *Open-Ended Evolution of Self-Improving Agents* contradicts Robeyns et al. (self-improving coding agent): concurrent hill-climbing approach; DGM Greedy ablation replicates it
- Belcak 2025, *Small Language Models are the Future of Agentic AI* contradicts Training compute-optimal LLMs (Hoffmann et al.) (`2203.15556`): scaling-law counter-argument CA1, rebutted by A8-A9
- Belcak 2025, *Small Language Models are the Future of Agentic AI* contradicts Scaling laws for transfer (Hernandez et al.): scaling-law counter-argument CA1
- Guha 2025, *Open Thoughts* contradicts OpenR1 (Face): Appendix D.3: packing did not hurt here, in contrast with OpenR1's finding that it did
- Agrawal 2025, *GEPA* contradicts Wan et al. generalization gap study: GEPA instructions generalise better than exemplars, contrary to prior finding
- Team 2025, *GLM-4.5* contradicts DeepScaleR: multi-stage increasing-length RL found less effective than single-stage 64K
- Team 2025, *GLM-4.5* contradicts Fewer truncations improve language modeling (best-fit packing): not used in pre-training, since random truncation is data augmentation
- Team 2025, *GLM-4.5* contradicts MiniCPM (WSD schedule): WSD underfit on SimpleQA, MMLU in early experiments
- Kim 2025, *Towards a Science of Scaling Agent Systems* contradicts More agents is all you need, Li et al.: Paper opposes the 'more agents' claim for agentic tasks
- Kim 2025, *Towards a Science of Scaling Agent Systems* contradicts Scaling large language model-based multi-agent collaboration, Qian et al.: Proposed logistic scaling laws, characterized as showing no significant universal pattern
- Reimers 2019, *Sentence-BERT: Sentence Embeddings using Siamese BERT-Networks* contradicts Conneau et al. 2017 MAX pooling: MAX pooling hurt here under regression
- Black 2022, *GPT-NeoX-20B: An Open-Source Autoregressive Language Model* contradicts Deduplicating training data (Lee et al.): authors saw no benefit from avoiding dedup at 20B but concede it is hard to dismiss
- Wang 2022, *Super-NaturalInstructions: Generalization via Declarative Instructions on 1600+ NLP Tasks* contradicts ZeroPrompt (Xu et al.): claim that model size has little impact with many tasks
- Min 2022, *MetaICL: Learning to Learn In Context* contradicts FLAN (Wei et al.): multi-task 0-shot does not beat raw LMs at 770M
- Min 2022, *MetaICL: Learning to Learn In Context* contradicts T0 (Sanh et al.): same
- Perez 2023, *Discovering Language Model Behaviors with Model-Written Evaluations* contradicts Efrat and Levy, The Turking Test: they reported negative results generating evaluations with GPT-2
- Peng 2023, *RWKV: Reinventing RNNs for the Transformer Era* contradicts Scaling laws for neural language models (Kaplan et al.) (`2001.08361`): Kaplan reported LSTMs do not follow transformer log-log scaling; RWKV does
- Yang 2015, *WikiQA: A Challenge Dataset for Open-Domain Question Answering* contradicts What is the Jeopardy model? (Wang et al.): created QASENT, whose lexical-overlap selection the paper argues is biased
- Pennington 2014, *Glove: Global Vectors for Word Representation* contradicts Baroni et al. Dont count, predict!: argues prediction models better; GloVe argues the two are not fundamentally different
- Levesque 2011, *The Winograd Schema Challenge* contradicts Recognizing Textual Entailment (Dagan, Glickman, Magnini 2006): Argued RTE rests on a fuzzy entailment notion and tests shallow inference
- Sutton 1999, *Policy Gradient Methods for Reinforcement Learning with Function Approximation* contradicts Baird and Moore, VAPS: VAPS does not climb the performance gradient so does not converge to a local optimum
- Moseley 2006, *Out of the Tar Pit* contradicts No Silver Bullet (Brooks): Adopts essence/accident vocabulary but denies most complexity is essential
- ?, *ch15-neuroscience* contradicts O'Reilly and Frank 2006: dopamine as RPE but not TD error
- ?, *Deciphering Data Architectures Choosi... (Z-Library)* contradicts Gartner data fabric definition: Serra diverges on virtualization being major and on intelligent fabric feasibility
- ?, *ch01-dimensional-modeling-primer* contradicts 3NF / entity-relationship modeling: Rejected for the presentation area; accepted only in staging
- ?, *ch05-bad-judges-significance* contradicts Taylor, An Introduction to Error Analysis: textbook that teaches judging by eye
- ?, *A Sober Look at Clustering Stability* contradicts Ben-Hur, Elisseeff, Guyon: A stability based method for discovering structure in clustered data: Stability-based model selection for k that this paper critiques.
- ?, *A Sober Look at Clustering Stability* contradicts Lange, Roth, Braun, Buhmann: Stability-based validation of clustering solutions: Same practice critiqued.
- ?, *ch02-introducing-modalities* contradicts Learning Transferable Visual Models From Natural Language Supervision (CLIP) (`2103.00020`): Book gives >250M pairs; the paper says 400M.
- ?, *ch38-markov-decision-processes* contradicts Bartlett and Tewari 2009: Chapter says the proof of Theorem 4 is incorrect
- ?, *ch38-markov-decision-processes* contradicts Agrawal and Jia 2017: Chapter says the proof is not quite correct
- ?, *SMOTE: Synthetic Minority Over-sampling Technique* contradicts Ling & Li 1998: combined over-sampling with replacement and under-sampling without gain; SMOTE differs in over-sampling method
- ?, *The harm of class imbalance corrections for risk prediction models: illustration and simulation using logistic regression* contradicts He & Garcia, Learning from imbalanced data: Argues imbalance is not a pervasive problem, against their framing
- ?, *The harm of class imbalance corrections for risk prediction models: illustration and simulation using logistic regression* contradicts Fernandez et al., Learning from Imbalanced Data Sets: Same
- ?, *The harm of class imbalance corrections for risk prediction models: illustration and simulation using logistic regression* contradicts Wallace & Dahabreh, Improving class probability estimates for imbalanced data: Says its Brier-score reading of RUS improvement is wrong
- ?, *14 Asking a KG questions with natural language | Knowledge Graphs and LLMs in Action* contradicts Retrieval-augmented generation (as discussed in chapter 13 of the same book): Chapter contrasts its schema-based query approach with RAG and lists RAG's limits

## agentic-apps

- `1609.01454` Liu 2016, *Attention-Based Recurrent Neural Network Models for Joint Intent Detection and Slot Filling*: **uses-method** Neural machine translation by jointly learning to align and translate (Bahdanau et al.) (`1409.0473`), Sequence to sequence learning (Sutskever et al.), Adam (Kingma & Ba) (`1412.6980`), Recurrent neural network regularization (Zaremba et al.); **baseline** Guo et al., joint semantic utterance classification and slot filling with recursive NNs, Xu & Sarikaya, CNN TriCRF; **uses-data** Mesnil et al., RNNs for slot filling
- `1902.10909` ?, *BERT for Joint Intent Classification and Slot Filling*: **extends** BERT: pre-training of deep bidirectional transformers (`1810.04805`); **baseline** Slot-gated modeling for joint slot filling and intent prediction (Goo et al.), Attention-based RNN models for joint intent detection and slot filling (Liu and Lane) (`1609.01454`), Multi-domain joint semantic frame parsing using bi-directional RNN-LSTM (Hakkani-Tur et al.); **uses-data** ATIS (Tur et al.), Snips voice platform (Coucke et al.); **related** Zhou and Xu, end-to-end SRL with RNNs
- `1910.00486` Vlasov 2019, *Dialogue Transformers*: **baseline** Few-shot generalization across dialogue tasks (REDP); **extends** Attention is all you need (`1706.03762`); **uses-method** Henderson et al., Training neural response selection, StarSpace; **related** Sahay et al., intent, dialog policies and response adaptation, RavenClaw / Grosz and Sidner, Mehri et al. 2019 / Mehri and Eskenazi 2019, Mosig et al., Where is the context?; **uses-data** MultiWOZ 2.1
- `2001.09977` Adiwardana 2020, *Towards a Human-like Open-Domain Chatbot*: **uses-method** The Evolved Transformer, Attention is all you need (`1706.03762`), Adafactor, The curious case of neural text degeneration (`1904.09751`), Hierarchical Neural Story Generation (`1805.04833`), Distilling the knowledge in a neural network (`1503.02531`); **extends** Sequence to sequence learning with neural networks (`1409.3215`); **baseline** DialoGPT, Cleverbot, Mitsuku (Worswick 2018), XiaoIce (Zhou et al. 2018); **related** Li et al. 2016a diversity-promoting objective; **uses-data** Vinyals and Le 2015; **contradicts** Liu et al. 2016; Lowe et al. 2017
- `2004.09936` Bunk 2020, *DIET: Lightweight Language Understanding for Dialogue Systems*: **baseline** HERMIT NLU (Vanzo et al.), BERT for joint intent classification and slot filling (Chen et al.) (`1902.10909`); **uses-method** ConveRT (Henderson et al.), BERT (Devlin et al.) (`1810.04805`), GloVe (Pennington et al.); **uses-data** Benchmarking NLU services (Liu et al.), Slot-gated modeling (Goo et al.); **extends** Dialogue Transformers (Vlasov et al.) (`1910.00486`); **related** To tune or not to tune? (Peters et al.)
- `2006.14779` ?, *Does the Whole Exceed its Parts? The Effect of AI Explanations on Complementary Team Performance*: **contradicts** Lai and Tan; Lai et al. deceptive review studies, Zhang et al., confidence and explanation on income prediction; **related** Bucinca et al., Proxy Tasks and Subjective Measures Can Be Misleading, Green and Chen, algorithm-in-the-loop decision making, Bansal et al., Optimizing AI for Teamwork; **uses-method** Ribeiro et al., LIME, RoBERTa (Liu et al.); **uses-data** ReClor (Yu et al.)
